evidence, a finite_witness and no decisive missing fact. decision=dismiss requires
a grounded refutation or equivalent route (J6). Otherwise return uncertain and

invalidate a currently established obligation (J2).

group as blocked in the scope audit. It is reviewed in a separate turn; do not
add a decision for it here.

*/\n6211|\n6212|#include <immintrin.h>\n6213|#include <inttypes.h>\n6214|#include <string.h>\n6215|\n6216|/* Functions in this file look at most 3 pixels (a,b,c) to predict the 4th (d).\n6217| * Luckily, it's\n6303|     * predicted to be half of the pixel above it.  So again, this works\n6304|     * perfectly with our loop if we make sure a starts at zero.\n6305|     */\n6306|\n6307|    size_t rb = rowbytes;\n6308|\n6309|    const __m128i zero = _mm_setzero_si128();\n6310|\n6311|    __m128i b;\n6312|    __m128i a, d = zero;\n6313|\n6314|    while(rb >= 4)\n6315|    {\n6316|        __m128i avg;\n6317|               b = load4(prev);\n6318|        a = d; d = load4(row );\n6319|\n6320|        /* PNG requires a truncating average, so we can't just use _mm_avg_epu8 */\n6321|        avg = _mm_avg_epu8(a,b);\n6322|        /* ...but we can fix it up by subtracting off 1 if it rounded up. */\n6323|        avg = _mm_sub_epi8(avg, _mm_and_si128(_mm_xor_si128(a, b),\n6324|                                            _mm_set1_epi8(1)));\n6325|        d = _mm_add_epi8(d, avg);\n6326|        store3(row, d);\n6327|\n6328|        prev += 3;\n6329|        row  += 3;\n6330|        rb   -= 3;\n6331|    }\n6332|\n6333|    if(rb > 0)\n6334|    {\n6335|        __m128i avg;\n6336|               b = load3(prev);\n6337|        a = d; d = load3(row );\n6338|\n6339|        /* PNG requires a truncating average, so we can't just use _mm_avg_epu8 */\n6340|        avg = _mm_avg_epu8(a, b);\n6341|        /* ...but we can fix it up by subtracting off 1 if it rounded up. */\n6342|        avg = _mm_sub_epi8(avg, _mm_and_si128(_mm_xor_si128(a, b),\n6343|                                            _mm_set1_epi8(1)));\n6344|\n6345|        d = _mm_add_epi8(d, avg);\n6346|        store3(row, d);\n6347|    }\n6348|}\n6349|\n6350|static void defilter_avg4(size_t rowbytes, unsigned char *row, const unsigned char *prev)\n6351|{\n6352|    /* The Avg filter predicts each pixel as the (truncated) average of a and b.\n6353|     * There's no pixel to the left of the first pixel.  Luckily, it's\n6354|     * predicted to be half of the pixel above it. lanes. */\n6385|#if (SPNG_SSE >= 3) && !defined(_MSC_VER)\n6386|__attribute__((target(\"ssse3\")))\n6387|#endif\n6388|static __m128i abs_i16(__m128i x)\n6389|{\n6390|#if SPNG_SSE >= 3\n6391|    return _mm_abs_epi16(x);\n6392|#else\n6393|    /* Read this all as, return x<0 ? -x : x.\n6394|     * To negate two's complement, you flip all the bits then add 1.\n6395|     */\n6396|    __m128i is_negative defilter_paeth3(size_t rowbytes, unsigned char *row, const unsigned char *prev)\n6418|{\n6419|    /* Paeth tries to predict pixel d using the pixel to the left of it, a,\n6420|     * and two pixels from the previous row, b and c:\n6421|     *   prev: c b\n6422|     *   row:  a d\n6423|     * The Paeth function predicts d to be whichever of a, b, or c is nearest to\n6424|     * p=a+b-c.\n6425|     *\n6426|     * The first pixel has no left context, and so uses an Up filter, p = b.\n6427|     * This works naturally with our main loop's p = a+b-c if we force a and c\n6428|     * to zero.\n6429|     * Here we zero b and d, which become c and a respectively at the start of\n6430|     * the loop.\n6431|     */\n6432|    size_t rb = rowbytes;\n6433|    const __m128i zero = _mm_setzero_si128();\n6434|    __m128i c, b = zero,\n6435|            a, d = zero;\n6436|\n6437|    while(rb >= 4)\n6438|    {\n6439|        /* It's easiest to do this math (particularly, deal with */\n6451|        pb = _mm_sub_epi16(a, c);\n6452|\n6453|        /* (p-c) == (a+b-c - c) == (a+b-c-c) == (b-c)+(a-c) */\n6454|        pc = _mm_add_epi16(pa, pb);\n6455|\n6456|        pa = abs_i16(pa);
